**parameters**　[{"parameter": "inputSourceRasterOrFeatures", "label_zh": "輸入網格或圖徵源", "label_en": "Input Raster or Feature Sources", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input source locations. This is an image service or feature service identifying the cells or locations from or to which the allocation for every output cell location is calculated. For an image service, the input type can be integer or floating point. For a feature service, the input type can be point, line or polygon."}, {"parameter": "outputDistanceAllocationRasterName", "label_zh": "輸出距離分配網格名稱", "label_en": "Output Distance Allocation Raster Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output distance allocation raster service."}, {"parameter": "inputBarrierRasterOrFeatures", "label_zh": "輸入網格或圖徵障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Raster or Feature Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The dataset that defines the barriers. The barriers can be defined by an integer or a floating-point image service, or by a feature service. For a feature service, the input type can be point, line or polygon. For an image service barrier, the barrier must have a valid value, including zero, and the areas that are not barriers must be NoData."}, {"parameter": "inputSurfaceRaster", "label_zh": "輸入表面網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Surface Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "An image service defining the elevation values at each cell location. The values are used to calculate the actual surface distance covered when passing between cells."}, {"parameter": "inputCostRaster", "label_zh": "輸入成本網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Cost Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "An image service defining the impedance or cost to move planimetrically through each cell. The value at each cell location represents the cost-per-unit distance for moving through the cell. Each cell location value is multiplied by the cell resolution while also compensating for diagonal movement to obtain the total cost of passing through the cell. The values of the cost raster can be integer or floating point. Cost raster values that are negative or zero are invalid but will be treated as small positive cost values."}, {"parameter": "inputVerticalRaster", "label_zh": "輸入垂直網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Vertical Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "An image service defining the z-values for each cell location. The values are used for calculating the slope used to identify the vertical factor incurred when moving from one cell to another."}, {"parameter": "verticalFactor", "label_zh": "垂直系數 （可選）", "label_en": "Vertical Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Vertical Factor", "description_en": "Specifies the relationship between the vertical cost factor and the vertical relative moving angle (VRMA). There are several factors with modifiers that identify a defined vertical factor graph. The graphs are used to identify the vertical factor used in calculating the total cost of moving into a neighboring cell. In the descriptions below, vertical factor (VF) defines the vertical difficulty encountered in moving from one cell to the next, and vertical relative moving angle (VRMA) identifies the slope angle between the FROM or processing cell and the TO cell. The Vertical Factor options are as follows: Binary—If the VRMA is greater than the low-cut angle and less than the high-cut angle, the VF is set to the value associated with the zero factor; otherwise, it is infinity. Linear—The VF is a linear function of the VRMA. Symmetric Linear—The VF is a linear function of the VRMA in either the negative or positive side of the VRMA, and the two linear functions are symmetrical with respect to the VF (y) axis. Inverse Linear—The VF is an inverse linear function of the VRMA. Symmetric Inverse Linear—The VF is an inverse linear function of the VRMA in either the negative or positive side of the VRMA, and the two linear functions are symmetrical with respect to the VF (y) axis. Cos—The VF is the cosine-based function of the VRMA. Sec—The VF is the secant-based function of the VRMA. Cos-Sec—The VF is the cosine-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is negative and is the secant-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is not negative. Sec-Cos—The VF is the secant-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is negative and is the cosine-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is not negative. Hiking Time— Defines the relationship between the vertical cost factor and the vertical relative moving angle through the reciprocal of Tobler's function, which results in hiking time in hours. Bidirectional Hiking Time—Defines the relationship between the vertical cost factor and the vertical relative moving angle through a bidirectional hiking function. Modifiers to the vertical keywords are as follows: Zero factor—The vertical factor used when the VRMA is zero. This factor positions the y-intercept of the specified function. By definition, the zero factor is not applicable to any of the trigonometric vertical functions (COS, SEC, COS-SEC, or SEC-COS). The y-intercept is defined by these functions. Low Cut angle—The VRMA angle below which the VF will be set to infinity. High Cut angle—The VRMA angle above which the VF will be set to infinity. Slope—The slope of the straight line used with the Linear and Inverse Linear vertical factor keywords. The slope is specified as a fraction of rise over run (for example, 45 percent slope is 1/45, which is input as 0.02222)."}, {"parameter": "inputHorizontalRaster", "label_zh": "輸入水平網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Horizontal Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "An image service defining the horizontal direction at each cell. The values on the raster must be integers ranging from 0 to 360, with 0 degrees being north, or toward the top of the screen, and increasing clockwise. Flat areas should be given a value of -1. The values at each location will be used in conjunction with the Horizontal Factor parameter to determine the horizontal cost incurred when moving from a cell to its neighbors."}, {"parameter": "horizontalFactor", "label_zh": "水平系數 （可選）", "label_en": "Horizontal Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Horizontal Factor", "description_en": "Specifies the relationship between the horizontal cost factor and the horizontal relative moving angle (HRMA). There are several factors with modifiers that identify a defined horizontal factor graph. The graphs are used to identify the horizontal factor used in calculating the total cost of moving into a neighboring cell. In the descriptions below, horizontal factor (HF) defines the horizontal difficulty encountered when moving from one cell to the next, and horizontal relative moving angle (HRMA) identifies the angle between the horizontal direction from a cell and the moving direction. The Horizontal Factor options are as follows: Binary—If the HRMA is less than the cut angle, the HF is set to the value associated with the zero factor; otherwise, it is infinity. Forward—Only forward movement is allowed. The HRMA must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than 90 degrees (0 <= HRMA < 90). If the HRMA is greater than 0 and less than 45 degrees, the HF for the cell is set to the value associated with the zero factor. If the HRMA is greater than or equal to 45 degrees, the side value modifier value is used. The HF for any HRMA equal to or greater than 90 degrees is set to infinity. Linear—The HF is a linear function of the HRMA. Inverse Linear—The HF is an inverse linear function of the HRMA. Modifiers to the horizontal factors are as follows: Zero factor—The horizontal factor to be used when the HRMA is zero. This factor positions the y-intercept for any of the horizontal factor functions. Cut angle—The HRMA angle beyond which the HF will be set to infinity. Slope—The slope of the straight line used with the Linear and Inverse Linear horizontal factor keywords. The slope is specified as a fraction of rise over run (for example, 45 percent slope is 1/45, which is input as 0.02222). Side value—The HF when the HRMA is greater than or equal to 45 degrees and less than 90 degrees when the Forward horizontal factor keyword is specified."}, {"parameter": "outputDistanceAccumulationRasterName", "label_zh": "輸出距離累積網格名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Distance Accumulation Raster Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The output distance accumulation raster name. The distance accumulation raster contains the accumulative distance for each cell from, or to, the least-cost source."}, {"parameter": "outputBackDirectionRasterName", "label_zh": "輸出反向網格名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Back Direction Raster Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The output back direction raster name. The back direction raster contains calculated directions in degrees. The direction identifies the next cell along the optimal path back to the least accumulative cost source while avoiding barriers. The range of values is from 0 degrees to 360 degrees. The value 0 is reserved for the source cells. Due east (right) is 90 degrees, and the values increase clockwise (180 is south, 270 is west, and 360 is north). The output raster is of type float."}, {"parameter": "outputSourceDirectionRasterName", "label_zh": "輸出源方向網格名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Source Direction Raster Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The output source direction raster name. The source direction raster identifies the direction of the least accumulated cost source cell as an azimuth in degrees. The range of values is from 0 degrees to 360 degrees. The value 0 is reserved for the source cells. Due east (right) is 90 degrees, and the values increase clockwise (180 is south, 270 is west, and 360 is north). The output raster is of type float."}, {"parameter": "outputSourceLocationRasterName", "label_zh": "輸出源位置網格名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Source Location Raster Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The output source location raster name. The source location raster is a multiband output. The first band contains a row index, and the second band contains a column index. These indexes identify the location of the source cell that is the least accumulated cost distance away."}, {"parameter": "sourceField", "label_zh": "源欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field used to assign values to the source locations. It must be of type integer."}, {"parameter": "sourceInitialAccumulation", "label_zh": "初始累積 （可選）", "label_en": "Initial Accumulation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The initial accumulative cost that will be used to begin the cost calculation. Allows for the specification of the fixed cost associated with a source. Instead of starting at a cost of zero, the cost algorithm will begin with the value set by Initial accumulation. The values must be zero or greater. The default is 0."}, {"parameter": "sourceMaximumAccumulation", "label_zh": "最大累積 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Accumulation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The maximum accumulation for the traveler for a source. The cost calculations continue for each source until the specified accumulation is reached. The values must be greater than zero. The default accumulation is to the edge of the output raster."}, {"parameter": "sourceCostMultiplier", "label_zh": "成本倍數 （可選）", "label_en": "Cost Multiplier (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The multiplier that will be applied to the cost values. This allows for control of the mode of travel or the magnitude at a source. The greater the multiplier, the greater the cost to move through each cell. The values must be greater than zero. The default is 1."}, {"parameter": "sourceDirection", "label_zh": "行駛方向 （可選）", "label_en": "Travel Direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String; Field", "description_en": "Specifies the direction of the traveler when applying horizontal and vertical factors. If you select the String option, you can choose between from and to options, which will be applied to all sources. If you select the Field option, you can select the field from the source data that determines the direction to use for each source. The field must contain the text string FROM_SOURCE or TO_SOURCE. Travel from source—The horizontal factor and vertical factor will be applied beginning at the input source and travel out to the nonsource cells. This is the default. Travel to source—The horizontal factor and vertical factor will be applied beginning at each nonsource cell and travel back to the input source."}, {"parameter": "distanceMethod", "label_zh": "距離法 （可選）", "label_en": "Distance Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the distance will be calculated using a planar (flat earth) or a geodesic (ellipsoid) method. Planar—The distance calculation will be performed on a projected flat plane using a 2D Cartesian coordinate system. This is the default. Geodesic—The distance calculation will be performed on the ellipsoid. Regardless of input or output projection, the results will not change."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.ra.DistanceAllocation(inputSourceRasterOrFeatures, outputDistanceAllocationRasterName, {inputBarrierRasterOrFeatures}, {inputSurfaceRaster}, {inputCostRaster}, {inputVerticalRaster}, {verticalFactor}, {inputHorizontalRaster}, {horizontalFactor}, {outputDistanceAccumulationRasterName}, {outputBackDirectionRasterName}, {outputSourceDirectionRasterName}, {outputSourceLocationRasterName}, {sourceField}, {sourceInitialAccumulation}, {sourceMaximumAccumulation}, {sourceCostMultiplier}, {sourceDirection}, {distanceMethod})
- VfBinary({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- VfLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfSymLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfSymInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfCos({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {cosPower})
- VfSec({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {secPower})
- VfCosSec({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {cosPower}, {secPower})
- VfSecCos({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {secPower}, {cos_power})
- VfHikingTime({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- VfBidirHikingTime({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- HfBinary({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle})
- HfForward({zeroFactor}, {sideValue})
- HfLinear({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle}, {slope})
- HfInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle}, {slope})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：inputSourceRasterOrFeatures、outputDistanceAllocationRasterName、inputBarrierRasterOrFeatures、inputSurfaceRaster、inputCostRaster、inputVerticalRaster、verticalFactor、inputHorizontalRaster、horizontalFactor、outputDistanceAccumulationRasterName、outputBackDirectionRasterName、outputSourceDirectionRasterName、outputSourceLocationRasterName、sourceField、sourceInitialAccumulation、sourceMaximumAccumulation、sourceCostMultiplier、sourceDirection、distanceMethod。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 002966：ObjectID = 的記錄（位于 輸入資料集中）不含語言信息。 將使用針對“國家/地區”選擇的語言代碼：“”。 · 描述

**id**　1d1f73488d8ee9134d-1

**english**　002966: Record ObjectID = <value> from the '<value>' input dataset has no Language information. The value selected for Language Code will be used: '<value>'.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 2901-3000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002966.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002966.html

**text**　未針對參考資料中的每個圖徵定義有效的語言代碼值，例如 ENG 或 GER。 在使用多語言參考資料構建定位器時，語言代碼值至關重要，因此定位器中將包含特定于語言的拼寫規則和輸出格式，以防止在地理編碼結果中錯誤分配語言。
如果您通過針對語言代碼參數選擇 <按資料中所定義的方式> 選項定義了語言代碼，然后將資料中的欄位映射到欄位映射中的語言代碼定位器角色欄位，則對于資料集中的任何記錄，資料中的欄位都不能為空。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002966.html#description

## 002966：ObjectID = 的記錄（位于 輸入資料集中）不含語言信息。 將使用針對“國家/地區”選擇的語言代碼：“”。 · 解決方案

**id**　1d1f73488d8ee9134d-2

**english**　002966: Record ObjectID = <value> from the '<value>' input dataset has no Language information. The value selected for Language Code will be used: '<value>'.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 2901-3000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002966.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002966.html

**text**　如果資料中的所有記錄均使用同一語言，并且該語言位于語言代碼參數下拉列表中，請選擇此參數的語言，而非選擇 <按資料中所定義的方式>。 由此可將正確的語言代碼應用于資料中的每條記錄。
如果資料中的記錄使用多個語言，則必須將缺失的語言代碼添加到該欄位為空或缺失的記錄。 可在地理編碼覆蓋區域地圖中引用的受支持的語言代碼列中找到受支持的語言和代碼列表。

## 最優插值 (影像分析工具) · 匯總

**id**　1d20045ced48fafa87-1

**english**　Optimal Interpolation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 插值分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html

**text**　通過統計合并多個來源的資料生成輸出網格。 該工具可用于將背景資料（如模型輸出）與觀測點資料（如點測量）合并，進行插值操作。
了解有關“最優插值”工作原理的詳細信息。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#summary

## 最優插值 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1d20045ced48fafa87-2

**english**　Optimal Interpolation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 插值分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html

**text**　該工具接受背景資料集和觀測點資料集作為輸入。 輸入背景網格參數值通常是來自模型輸出的格網化網格，輸入觀測點資料參數值通常是圖徵或軌跡資料集，其中包含以點形式存在的觀測資料。
要合并資料集，需根據其相對精度為背景資料集和觀測點資料集分配權重，以最小化分析誤差方差。 相對精度是根據背景誤差方差、觀測點誤差方差和背景誤差相關長度參數值確定的，用于確定背景測量之間的背景誤差相關性。
通常情況下，背景誤差方差和觀測點誤差方差參數值通常是根據背景資料和觀測點資料估算得到的全局常數。 或者，您可以提供另一個具有相同行和列、像元大小和范圍的網格，以表示背景誤差方差。 您還可以提供觀測點資料中表示觀測點誤差方差的欄位。
背景誤差相關長度參數值用于估算觀測點對輸出的影響。 它以輸入背景資料的空間參考單位表示。 較大的值對遠離輸出像元的點具有較高的影響力。
假設觀測點誤差相關性為 0。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#usage

- 背景測量的誤差方差。 輸入可以是單個值或誤差方差網格。 如果提供單個值，該值將用作所有背景測量的誤差方差。 如果提供誤差方差網格，則背景資料中的每個像元將從相應的背景誤差方差網格獲取其誤差方差。 誤差方差網格必須具有與背景資料相同的像元大小和范圍。

## 最優插值 (影像分析工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　1d20045ced48fafa87-20

**english**　Optimal Interpolation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 插值分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import *

# Set local variables
in_background_raster = r"C:\data\global_ssh.tif"
in_obs_data = r"C:\data\jason_3_ssh.shp"
obs_field = "SSH"
background_error_var = 0.1
obs_error_var = 0.2
background_error_corr_length = 100 # km

# Run
output = arcpy.ia.OptimalInterpolation(in_background_raster, in_obs_data, obs_field, background_error_var, obs_error_var, background_error_corr_length)
output.save(r"c:\output\OI_ssh.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#return-value

## 最優插值 (影像分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 插值分析工具集

**english**　Optimal Interpolation

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html

**id**　1d20045ced48fafa87-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Optimal Interpolation，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入背景網格 [in_background_raster]、輸入觀測點資料 [in_obs_data]、觀測點欄位 [obs_field]、背景誤差方差 [background_error_var]、觀測點誤差方差 [obs_error_var]、背景誤差相關長度 （可選） [background_error_corr_length]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#parameters

## 最優插值 (影像分析工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Optimal Interpolation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 插值分析工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html

**id**　1d20045ced48fafa87-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/image-analyst/optimal-interpolation.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_background_raster", "label_zh": "輸入背景網格", "label_en": "Input Background Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Dataset; Raster Layer; Image Service", "description_en": "The input background raster also known as the background field."}, {"parameter": "in_obs_data", "label_zh": "輸入觀測點資料", "label_en": "Input Observation Data", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer; Trajectory Layer", "description_en": "The input point features that will be used for interpolation."}, {"parameter": "obs_field", "label_zh": "觀測點欄位", "label_en": "Observation Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The field containing observation values that will be used for interpolation."}, {"parameter": "background_error_var", "label_zh": "背景誤差方差", "label_en": "Background Error Variance", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Double; Raster Dataset; Raster Layer; Image Service", "description_en": "The error variance of the background measurements. The input can be a single value or an error variance raster. If a single value is provided, the value will be used as the error variance for all background measurements. If an error variance raster is provided, each cell in the background data will obtain its error variance from the corresponding background error variance raster. The error variance raster must have the same cell size and extent as the background data."}, {"parameter": "obs_error_var", "label_zh": "觀測點誤差方差", "label_en": "Observation Error Variance", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Double; String", "description_en": "The error variance of the observations. The input can be a single value or a field from the observation data. If a single value is provided, the value will be used as the error variance for all observations. If a field in the observation data is provided, values in the field will be used as the error variance for each corresponding observation point."}, {"parameter": "background_error_corr_length", "label_zh": "背景誤差相關長度 （可選）", "label_en": "Background Error Correlation Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The correlation length between background measurements. The default is three times the cell size of the Input Background Raster parameter value."}]

**signatures**　
- OptimalInterpolation(in_background_raster, in_obs_data, obs_field, background_error_var, obs_error_var, {background_error_corr_length})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_background_raster、in_obs_data、obs_field、background_error_var、obs_error_var、background_error_corr_length。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 增密采樣網絡 (地統計分析工具) · 匯總

**id**　1d5cd64bf6c0aaf7c8-1

**english**　Densify Sampling Network

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 采樣網絡設計工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html

**text**　使用預定義的地統計克里金圖層來確定新監測站的構建位置。 也可用于確定哪些監測站應從現有網絡中移除。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#summary

## 增密采樣網絡 (地統計分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1d5cd64bf6c0aaf7c8-2

**english**　Densify Sampling Network

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 采樣網絡設計工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html

**text**　輸入地統計圖層必須是克里金圖層。
請求生成多個新位置時只生成了一個新位置的情況可能會出現。 當根據選擇條件持續選擇同一新位置時，就會出現這種情況。 通過指定限制性距離參數的值，可以防止出現這種情況。 當選擇條件為下限四分位數閾值或上限四分位數閾值（Python 中的 QUARTILE_THRESHOLD 或 QUARTILE_THRESHOLD_UPPER）時，使用限制性距離尤為重要。
為了判斷哪些位置對預測表面的影響最小，可以將用于創建克里金圖層的圖徵類作為輸入候選點圖徵參數的值。 如果要停用某些監測站，則影響最小的位置是良好的候選移除點。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#usage

- 采樣網絡的增密方法。 預測的標準誤差選項會向預測標準誤差較大的位置分配額外的權重。 如果正在研究的變量存在臨界閾值（例如可接受的最高臭氧含量），則標準誤差閾值、下限四分位數閾值和上限四分位數閾值選項將非常有用。 標準誤差閾值選項會向值接近于閾值的位置分配額外的權重。 下限四分位數閾值選項會向最不可能超出臨界閾值的位置分配額外的權重。 上限四分位數閾值選項會向最有可能超出臨界閾值的位置分配額外的權重。 當選擇條件設置為標準誤差閾值、下限四分位數閾值或上限四分位數閾值時，閾值參數將變為可用，以便您指定感興趣的閾值。 每個選項的公式為： <!--```nohighlight 預測的標準誤差 = stderr 標準誤差閾值 = stderr(s)(1 - 2 · abs(prob[Z(s) > threshold] - 0.5)) 下限四分位數閾值 = (Z0.75(s) - Z0.25(s)) · (prob[Z(s) < threshold]) 上限四分位數閾值 = (Z0.75(s) - Z0.25(s)) · (prob[Z(s) > threshold]) <div data-domains="selection_criteria"> - <span data-name="STDERR">**預測的標準誤差**</span>—<span data-desc="STDERR">預測的標準誤差條件</span> - <span data-name="STDERR_THRESHOLD">**標準誤差閾值**</span>—<span data-desc="STDERR_THRESHOLD">標準誤差閾值條件</span> - <span data-name="QUARTILE_THRESHOLD">**下限四分位數閾值**</span>—<span data-desc="QUARTILE_THRESHOLD">下限四分位數閾值條件</span> - <span data-name="QUARTILE_THRESHOLD_UPPER">**上限四分位數閾值**</span>—<span data-desc="QUARTILE_THRESHOLD_UPPER">上限四分位數閾值條件</span> </div>
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#parameters

**id**　1d5cd64bf6c0aaf7c8-24

**english**　Densify Sampling Network

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

- 地統計分析工具箱
- 采樣網絡設計工具集

**text**　# Name: DensifySamplingNetwork_Example_02.py
# Description: Densify a sampling network based on a predefined geostatistical
#              kriging layer. It uses, inter alia, the Standard Error of
#              Prediction map to determine where new locations are required.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inLayer = "C:/gapyexamples/data/Kriging.lyr"
numberPoints = 2
outPoints = "C:/gapyexamples/output/outDSN"

# Run DensifySamplingNetworks
arcpy.ga.DensifySamplingNetwork(inLayer, numberPoints, outPoints)

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#parameters

## 增密采樣網絡 (地統計分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 采樣網絡設計工具集

**english**　Densify Sampling Network

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html

**id**　1d5cd64bf6c0aaf7c8-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Densify Sampling Network，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入地統計圖層 [in_geostat_layer]、輸出點數 [number_output_points]、輸出點圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、選擇條件 （可選） [selection_criteria]、閾值 （可選） [threshold]、輸入權重網格 （可選） [in_weight_raster]、輸入候選點圖徵 （可選） [in_candidate_point_features]、限制性距離 （可選） [inhibition_distance]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/densify-sampling-network.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_geostat_layer", "label_zh": "輸入地統計圖層", "label_en": "Input geostatistical layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Geostatistical Layer", "description_en": "Input a geostatistical layer resulting from a Kriging model."}, {"parameter": "number_output_points", "label_zh": "輸出點數", "label_en": "Number of output points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "Specify how many sample locations to generate."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出點圖徵類", "label_en": "Output point feature class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The name of the output feature class."}, {"parameter": "selection_criteria", "label_zh": "選擇條件 （可選）", "label_en": "Selection criteria (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Methods to densify a sampling network. The Standard error of prediction option will give extra weight to locations where the standard error of prediction is large. The Standard error threshold, Lower quartile threshold, and Upper quartile threshold options are useful when there is a critical threshold value for the variable under study (such as the highest admissible ozone level). The Standard error threshold option will give extra weight to locations whose values are close to the threshold. The Lower quartile threshold option will give extra weight to locations that are least likely to exceed the critical threshold. The Upper quartile threshold option will give extra weight to locations that are most likely to exceed the critical threshold. When the Selection criteria is set to Standard error threshold, Lower quartile threshold, or Upper quartile threshold, the Threshold value parameter will become available, allowing you specify your threshold of interest. The equations for each option are: Standard error of prediction = stderr Standard error threshold = stderr(s)(1 - 2 · abs(prob[Z(s) > threshold] - 0.5)) Lower quartile threshold = (Z<sub>0.75</sub>(s) - Z<sub>0.25</sub>(s)) · (prob[Z(s) < threshold]) Upper quartile threshold = (Z<sub>0.75</sub>(s) - Z<sub>0.25</sub>(s)) · (prob[Z(s) > threshold]) Standard error of prediction—Standard error of prediction criteria Standard error threshold—Standard error threshold criteria Lower quartile threshold—Lower quartile threshold criteria Upper quartile threshold—Upper quartile threshold criteria"}, {"parameter": "threshold", "label_zh": "閾值 （可選）", "label_en": "Threshold value (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The threshold value used to densify the sampling network. This parameter is only applicable when Standard error threshold, Lower quartile threshold, or Upper quartile threshold selection criteria is used."}, {"parameter": "in_weight_raster", "label_zh": "輸入權重網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input weight raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "A raster used to determine which locations to weight for preference."}, {"parameter": "in_candidate_point_features", "label_zh": "輸入候選點圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Input candidate point features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "Sample locations to pick from."}, {"parameter": "inhibition_distance", "label_zh": "限制性距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Inhibition distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "Used to prevent any samples being placed within this distance from each other."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.ga.DensifySamplingNetwork(in_geostat_layer, number_output_points, out_feature_class, {selection_criteria}, {threshold}, {in_weight_raster}, {in_candidate_point_features}, {inhibition_distance})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_geostat_layer、number_output_points、out_feature_class、selection_criteria、threshold、in_weight_raster、in_candidate_point_features、inhibition_distance。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 生成趨勢網格 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1d7e9a46511debb55f-2

**english**　Generate Trend Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html

**text**　該工具的一個應用示例是：假設您擁有 40 年的月度海洋溫度資料，并希望為每個像素擬合一條趨勢線，以觀察溫度隨時間的變化趨勢及其具體變化情況。
受支持的多維網格資料集包括云網格格式 (CRF)、多維鑲嵌資料集或者由 netCDF、GRIB 或 HDF 格式文件生成的多維網格圖層。
該工具將生成云網格格式 (CRF) 的多維網格資料集。 目前，尚不支持其他輸出格式。
默認情況下，將使用 LZ77 壓縮類型對多維網格輸出進行壓縮。 但是，建議將壓縮類型更改為 LERC 并根據資料來調整最大誤差值。 例如，如果您希望分析結果精確到三位小數，對于最大誤差值，請使用 0.001。 避免不必要的精度要求，因為它們將增加處理時間和存儲大小。
該工具可用于沿線性、諧波或多項式趨勢線擬合資料，也可用于使用 Mann-Kendall 或 Seasonal-Kendall 測試執行趨勢檢測。
Mann-Kendall 和 Seasonal-Kendall 測試可用于確定資料中是否存在單調性趨勢。 上述測試為非參數測試，這意味著它們不會假設具體的資料分布。Mann-Kendall 測試不考慮序列相關性或季節性影響。 如果資料為季節性資料，則 Seasonal-Kendall 測試更為適合。
如果使用該工具執行 Mann-Kendall 或 Seasonal-Kendall 測試，則輸出將為五波段網格，如下所示： 波段 1 = Sen 斜率 波段 2 = p 值 波段 3 = Mann-Kendall 得分 (S) 波段 4 = S 方差 波段 5 = Z 得分 Mann-Kendall 或 Seasonal-Kendall 測試的輸出可用于確定多維時間序列中的哪些像素具有統計顯著性趨勢。 您可以將此信息與線性、諧波或多項式趨勢分析結合使用，以提取時間序列中的重要趨勢。 您可以生成包含像素（像素帶有顯著 p 值）的掩膜，將該掩膜應用于多維網格，以及將此掩膜多維網格用作工具的輸入，以執行線性、諧波或多項式趨勢分析。
系統提供三個趨勢線選項用于沿維度擬合變量值的趨勢：線性、諧波和多項式。 三個趨勢擬合選項如下所述。 Linear—線性趨勢線是最佳擬合直線，可用于估計簡單的線性關系。 線性趨勢可突出顯示以穩定速率增加或減少的變化率。 線性趨勢線的公式如下： y = 像素變量值 x = 維度值 ß0 = y 截距 ß1 = 線性斜率或變化率 ß1 > 0 表示增加趨勢 ß1 &lt; 0 表示減少趨勢 Harmonic—諧波趨勢線是周期性重復的曲線，最適用于描述遵循周期性模式的資料，例如季節性溫度變化。 諧波趨勢線的公式如下： y = 像素變量值 t = 儒略日 ß0 = y 截距 ß1 = 變化率 α, γ = 年際或年內變化的系數 ω = i f = 諧波頻率 Polynomial—多項式趨勢線是一條曲線，可用于波動的資料。 在這種情況下，多項式的階數值用于指示發生的最大波動數。 多項式趨勢線的公式如下： y = 像素變量值 x = 維度值 ß0, ß1, ß2, ß3, ..., ßn = 常系數

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html#usage

## 生成趨勢網格 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1d7e9a46511debb55f-3

**english**　Generate Trend Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html

**text**　如果該工具用于線性、諧波或多項式趨勢分析，輸出趨勢網格可以作為使用趨勢網格預測工具的輸入。 趨勢網格屬于多維網格，其中每個剖切均是一個包含趨勢線相關信息的多波段網格。 如果要分析包含單個維度（例如，時間）的資料集的單個變量趨勢，則輸出資料集中將具有單個剖切。 如果要分析包含多個維度（例如，時間和深度）的資料集的單個變量，則每個剖切將包含沿分析中未包含維度的每個維度值的趨勢信息。 對于線性趨勢分析，輸出包含三波段網格，其中： 波段 1 = 斜率 波段 2 = 截距 波段 3 = 均方根誤差 (RMSE) 或最佳擬合線周圍的誤差 對于諧波趨勢分析，輸出中的波段數取決于諧波頻率。 當頻率設置為 1 時，輸出將為五波段網格，如下所示： 波段 1 = 斜率 波段 2 = 截距 波段 3 = Harmonic_sin1 波段 4 = Harmonic_cos1 波段 5 = RMSE 當頻率設置為 2 時，輸出將為七波段網格，如下所示 波段 1 = 斜率 波段 2 = 截距 波段 3 = Harmonic_sin1 波段 4 = Harmonic_cos1 波段 5 = Harmonic_sin2 波段 6 = Harmonic_cos2 波段 7 = RMSE 對于多項式趨勢分析，輸出中的波段數取決于多項式階數。 二階多項式擬合將生成四波段網格，如下所示： 波段 1 = Polynomial_2 波段 2 = Polynomial_1 波段 3 = Polynomial_0 波段 4 = RMSE 三階多項式擬合將生成五波段網格，如下所示： 波段 1 = Polynomial_3 波段 2 = Polynomial_2 波段 3 = Polynomial_1 波段 4 = Polynomial_0 波段 5 = RMSE
諧波趨勢分析的周期長度參數用于表示您希望在一天或全年的資料中看到的周期數量和周期長度。 例如，如果您希望資料在一年中經歷兩個變化周期，則周期長度將為 182.5 天或 0.5 年。 如果您每三小時采集一次溫度資料，并且每天存在一個變化周期，則周期長度將為 1 天。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html#usage

## 生成趨勢網格 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1d7e9a46511debb55f-4

**english**　Generate Trend Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/generate-trend-raster.html

**text**　諧波趨勢分析的頻率參數用于描述諧波模型以擬合至資料。 如果頻率設置為 1，則將使用線性和一階諧波曲線的組合來擬合模型。 如果頻率為 2，則將使用線性、一階諧波和二階諧波曲線的組合來擬合資料。 如果頻率為 3，則將使用附加的三階諧波曲線對資料進行建模，依此類推。
可以生成模型擬合優度統計資料作為線性、諧波或多項式趨勢擬合的可選輸出。 可以計算均方根誤差 (RMSE)、R 平方和趨勢斜率 p 值并顯示在輸出網格統計資料部分的屬性窗口中。 也可以使用 RGB 符號表示輸出趨勢網格，并將統計資料指定為紅色、綠色、藍色波段來顯示統計資料。
